Smooth objective functions for p:
```
def smooth_obj_grad_p(p, beta, n):
   q=convolve_self(p)
   qmax=q.max()
   # logsumexp safe
   exparg=beta*(q-qmax)
   # if beta large, use max 0; sumexp = exp(sum(log(exparg)))? Use np.logsumexp? We can avoid scipy.special by manual:
   # exparg max 0; sum_exp = np.exp(exparg).sum()
   # if underflow all? at least one exp 1.
   sum_exp = np.exp(np.clip(exparg, -745, 0)).sum()
   L = (qmax + np.log(sum_exp)) / beta
   weights = np.exp(exparg) / sum_exp
   c = cross_corr(p, weights)
   grad = 2*c
   return L, grad, qmax
```
Since q length 2n-1 up to 8192, exp OK.

def smooth_obj_grad_z(z, beta):
   p = softmax(z)
   L, grad_p, qmax = smooth_obj_grad_p(p, beta)
   grad_z = p*(grad_p - np.dot(grad_p,p))
   return L, grad_z
```

```
def lowdim_objective_grad_xparam(c, xp, x, beta, n, grad_offset):
    v = np.interp(x, xp, c)
    p = softmax(v)
    L, grad_p, qmax = smooth_obj_grad_p(p, beta, n)
    grad_v = p*(grad_p - np.dot(grad_p,p))
    # accumulate to controls
    # x in [0,1]; xp = linspace(0,1,m)
    # For interior points not exactly xp:
    # idx = floor(x*(m-1)) for x<1; t=x*(m-1)-idx
    idx = np.minimum(np.floor(x*(m-1)).astype(int), m-2)
    t = x*(m-1)-idx
    grad_c = np.zeros(m)
    np.add.at(grad_c, idx, (1-t)*grad_v)
    np.add.at(grad_c, idx+1, t*grad_v)
    return L, grad_c, p, qmax
```

def multiplicative_step(p, q, weights, step):
    g = 2 * cross_corr(p, weights)
    d = g - np.dot(g, p)  # gradient in log p (before multiplying by p) ???
    # For softmax z gradient = p*d. Multiplicative exponent should use d (or g - mean_w)
    d = d - d.mean()? Since adding constant to d only scales p. Use centering by arithmetic mean or dot? For renormalization, constant cancels. Use d - d.mean() to avoid overflow. But dot(g,p) weighted? If we use d = g - np.mean(g), not exact z gradient. But any mean subtraction okay for multiplicative. Choose arithmetic mean to remove large constant when p not uniform.
    scale = max(abs(d.max()), abs(d.min()))
    exp_arg = -step * d / scale
    p_new = p * np.exp(exp_arg)
    p_new /= p_new.sum()
```

Let's implement LAD robustly:
```
def lad_step(p, q, qmax, K, eps_active, eta, scale_noise=0.0):
    L = len(q)
    weights = np.full(L, eps_active)
    # top K indices: if K>=L, all uniform? else
    if K < L:
        idx = np.argpartition(q, -K)[-K:]
    else:
        idx = np.arange(L)
    weights[idx] += 1.0
    # Optionally weight top by q-values? We can scale top additions by exp? But uniform top enough.
    # If qmax large, maybe weight top with descending? Could normalize top by q / qmax? Use top vals normalized to ensure stronger for max.
    # Better: addition = max(q[idx],0)/qmax? Since all near qmax, similar.
    weights /= weights.sum()
    g = 2.0 * cross_corr(p, weights)
    d = g - np.dot(g, p)
    d = d - d.mean()
    scale = max(abs(d.max()), abs(d.min()), 1e-14)
    # log update
    arg = -eta * d / scale
    p_new = p * np.exp(arg)
    s = p_new.sum()
    if s<=0: p_new = p.copy()
    else: p_new /= s
    return p_new
```
But if p has exact zeros, they remain. Start with floor `p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300/n?)` and after updates maybe zeros. Use floor at end? Since many zeros may hurt; can re-floor small: `p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)` and renorm. Or `p = (1-beta)*p + beta/n` periodically? Could reintroduce zeros. But exact zeros maybe locally feasible; if initial no zeros, exp won't create exact zeros unless underflow. We can keep p positive by adding tiny: after update `p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300); p/=p.sum()`.

         best_score=sc; best_p=p_c
         if sc < target: break
   return best_p,best_score
```

Pseudo:
```
def run_lad(p, time_limit, target_score, rng, verbose=False):
    n=len(p); p=normalize(p)
    start=time.monotonic(); best_p=p; best_score=score_normalized(p)
    K = 16; eta = 0.5; eps_active=1e-4
    no_improve=0; last_best=start; restart_count=0
    iter=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_limit:
        q=conv(p); qmax=q.max(); score=2*n*qmax
        if score < best_score: update
        iter +=1
        # adapt K: if no_improve > 500: K=min(512, K*2); if >2000: no_improve reset? Also if iter%... add noise
        weights = make_weights(q,K,eps_active)
        g=2*cross(p,weights)
        d=g-dot(g,p); d-=mean; scale=maxabs
        if scale < 1e-12: # converged, perturb
             p = add_log_noise(p, rng, 0.1); continue
        # try eta
        accepted=False
        # generate candidate with eta
        for ee in [eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*2?]:
           p_c = p * exp(-ee*d/scale); p_c=max(p_c, tiny); p_c/=sum
           score_c = 2*n*conv(p_c).max()
           if score_c < score* (1-1e-9) or score_c < best_score:
               if score_c < best_score: update best
               p=p_c; accepted=True; last_best=time.monotonic()
               if score_c < best_score*0.99? no_improve=0 else no_improve++?
               # adapt eta: if accepted at larger, keep; maybe increase slowly
               eta = min(5.0, ee*1.05)
               break
        if not accepted:
           no_improve +=1
           eta *=0.7
           if no_improve>100: K=min(1024,K*2); eps_active=max(1e-5,eps_active*0.5)
           if no_improve>1000:
               p = add_log_noise(p, rng, 0.05)
               no_improve=0
               eta=0.5
        if iter % 1000 ==0: print score
    return best_p,best_score
```

`fft_conv_self(a)`:
```
def fft_conv_self(a):
   n=len(a)
   if n==1: return np.array([a[0]*a[0]])
   size = 2*n-1
   fsize = 1 << (size-1).bit_length()
   A = fft(a, fsize)
   c = np.real(ifft(A*A))
   # set tiny negatives to 0? For max no issue, but weights maybe. Use c.clip(min=0)
   return c[:size]
```
For p sum1, max positive. Clip negative can alter sum? not important. Use clip to 0 for weights to avoid negative weights. But if all negative due to underflow? no.
`cross_corr(a,w)`:
```
def cross_corr(a,w):
   n=len(a); L=len(w)
   fsize=1 << (n+L-2).bit_length()
   A=fft(a,fsize); W=fft(w,fsize)
   return np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:n]
```
Need ensure linear cross no wrap: fsize ≥ n+L-1? For values c_i=∑_{k=0}^{L-1} w_k a_{k-i}, valid if k from i to i+n-1. This is convolution? We can verify with shape 2. a=[a0,a1], w=[w0,w1,w2]. fsize=4. W*conj(A) inverse gives c_i=∑ w_k a_{k-i mod4}. For i=0: k=0 a0 + k=1 a1? + k=2 a_{2 out of range}; But desired c0=∑_k w_k a_{k} for k=0,1 = w0a0+w1a1. Good if out of range zero. For i=1: k=1 w1a0? k? desired c1=w1 a0 + w2 a1. Inverse gives k=1 a0, k=2 a1, k=0 a_{-1}=a3 padded zero. Good. fsize must be ≥ n+L-1=4. OK.

if p.max()<=0: return uniform

p = np.exp(z)

return p
```

```
def resample(a, n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).reshape(-1)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-12: return np.ones(n)/n
   m=len(a)
   if m==n: p=a/s
   elif m==1: p=np.full(n,a[0])/n? sum normalize
   else:
      x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      p=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a/s, left=0.0, right=0.0)
      p=np.maximum(p,0)
      ps=p.sum()
      if ps<...: uniform
      p/=ps
   return p
```

Let's design `run_lad` more concretely:
```
def run_lad(p_init, time_limit, target_score, rng, symmetric=False):
    n=len(p_init)
    p=normalize_sum(p_init,1.0).astype(float)
    start=time.monotonic()
    q=conv(p); best_score=2*n*q.max(); best_p=p.copy(); current_score=best_score
    K = min(64, max(16, n//128))
    eps=1e-4
    eta=0.5
    no_improve=0
    last_change_iter=0
    iter=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_limit:
        iter +=1
        q=conv(p); current_score=2*n*q.max()
        if current_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score=current_score; best_p=p.copy(); no_improve=0
        if best_score < target_score:
            # maybe still run? break if time? We can break to save
            break
        if iter % 1000 == 0:
            print(...)
        # If stuck, perturb
        if no_improve > 2000:
            sigma=0.05 + 0.1*(no_improve//5000)
            p = add_log_noise(best_p, rng, min(sigma,1.0))
            no_improve=500
            eta=0.5
            K=min(512, K*2)
            eps=min(1e-2, eps*2)
            continue
        if no_improve > 500:
            K=min(1024, K*2)
            eps = max(1e-6, eps*0.3)
        elif no_improve > 200 and eps >1e-3:
            eps *=0.7
        weights=make_weights(q,K,eps)
        g=2*cross_corr(p,weights)
        d=g - np.dot(g,p)
        d = d - d.mean() # remove constant
        scale = max(float(np.max(np.abs(d))), 1e-14)
        if scale < 1e-14:
            no_improve +=100; continue
        # line search
        accepted=False
        current_iter_score = current_score
        e=eta
        for _ in range(4):
            p_c = p * np.exp(-e*d/scale)
            p_c = np.maximum(p_c, 1e-300)
            sc_sum=p_c.sum()
            if sc_sum <=0: break
            p_c /= sc_sum
            if symmetric: p_c=(p_c+p_c[::-1])/2; p_c/=p_c.sum()
            q_c=conv(p_c)
            score_c=2*n*q_c.max()
            if score_c < current_score - 1e-12 or score_c < best_score - 1e-12:
                p=p_c; current_score=score_c; accepted=True
                if score_c < best_score:
                    best_score=score_c; best_p=p_c.copy(); no_improve=0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1  # improved current but not best? Actually if score_c<current but >best, still progress
                # adapt eta
                if score_c < current_score* (1-1e-4): e = min(3.0, e*1.2)
                else: e = min(3.0, e*1.05)
                break
            e *= 0.5
            if e < 0.01: break
        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            eta = max(0.05, eta*0.8)
        # else set updated eta
        if accepted:
            eta = max(0.05, eta*1.02)  # maybe not e? use e? use eta=e?
            eta = min(3.0, e if accepted else eta)
    return best_p,best_score
```

def make_weights_exp(q, K):
   L=len(q); qmax=q.max()
   if K<=0: uniform
   K=min(K,L)
   if K >= L: uniform
   kth_val = np.partition(q, L-K)[L-K]  # Kth largest? Use argpartition maybe to avoid extra sort
   delta = max(qmax - kth_val, 1e-12)
   T = max(delta / 1.0, 1e-12)  # maybe
   exparg = (q - qmax)/T
   w = np.exp(np.clip(exparg, -50, 0))
   # add tiny floor
   w += 1e-6
   w /= w.sum()
```

       # if all? use softmax with temp qmax*(L/10)? but just uniform over top? no

   order = np.argpartition(q, L-K)[L-K:]  # top K? For K=1, L-1 index max.

`softmax_values(v)`:
```
def softmax_values(v):
   v = np.asarray(v, float)
   vmax = v.max()
   if not np.isfinite(vmax): return uniform? 
   exp = np.exp(np.clip(v-vmax, -745, 709))
   s=exp.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.full(len(v),1/len(v))
   return exp/s
```

`smooth_obj_z`:
```
def smooth_obj_z(z, beta, n):
    p=softmax_values(z)
    q=fft_conv_self(p)
    qmax=q.max()
    # L = (qmax + logsumexp(beta(q-qmax)))/beta
    exparg = beta*(q-qmax)
    exp_bc = np.exp(np.clip(exparg, -745, 0))
    sum_exp = exp_bc.sum()
    if sum_exp <=0: sum_exp=1
    obj=(qmax+np.log(sum_exp))/beta
    w=exp_bc/sum_exp
    c=cross_corr(p,w)
    gp=2*c
    dot=np.dot(gp,p)
    gz=p*(gp-dot)
    return obj,gz
```
Need if beta huge and qmax variation, logsumexp maybe beta*qmax... We shifted. ok.

`lbfgs_fun` and `lbfgs_jac`:

def lbfgs_fun(z, beta, n): return smooth_obj_z(z,beta,n)[0]
def lbfgs_jac(z, beta, n): return smooth_obj_z(z,beta,n)[1]
```
No closure.

`run_lbfgs_polish`:
```
def run_lbfgs_polish(p_init, time_limit, beta_schedule, maxiter, verbose=True):
   start=time.monotonic()
   n=len(p_init)
   best=normalize_sum(p_init); best_score=score_normalized(best)
   z=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)); z-=np.mean(z)
   for beta in beta_schedule:
      if time.monotonic()-start > time_limit-10: break
      t0=time.monotonic()
      try:
         res=scipy_minimize(lbfgs_fun, z, jac=lbfgs_jac, args=(beta,n), method='L-BFGS-B', options=dict(maxiter=maxiter, maxfun=maxiter*3, ftol=1e-15, gtol=1e-8, maxls=20, iprint=-1))
      except Exception as e:
         print('lbfgs error',e); break
      z=res.x
      p=softmax_values(z); p=normalize_sum(p)
      s=score_normalized(p)
      if s<best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=s
      z-=z.mean()
      print('beta', beta, 'score', best_score, 'time', ...)
   return best,best_score
```
`maxfun` maybe maxiter*4. L-BFGS may evaluate many. Use `maxiter=40`, `maxfun=200`. Time limit checks not inside scipy; could overrun. Since each call seconds, okay but need avoid >time_limit. ```
def make_weights_exp(q, K, floor_eps):
    L=q.size
    if L<=1: return np.ones(L)
    qmax=float(q.max())
    if qmax <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(L, 1.0/L)
    K=int(max(1, min(K, L)))
    if K >= L:
        top_min = q.min()
    else:
        # We need (L-K)-th smallest? Kth largest. np.partition(q, L-K)[L-K]
        kth = np.partition(q, L-K)[L-K]  # if L-K =0? K=L handled
        top_min = float(kth)
    delta = qmax - top_min
    # effective temperature: top_min gets exp(-0.5) maybe T=2*delta? If delta=0, use small
    if delta <= 1e-15 * qmax:
        T = max(qmax / (10.0*L), 1e-300)
    else:
        T = max(2.0 * delta, qmax / (100.0*K), 1e-300)
    exparg = (q - qmax) / T
    w = np.exp(np.clip(exparg, -700.0, 0.0))
    if floor_eps > 0:
       w = w + floor_eps
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0: return uniform
    return w/s
```

Exponential for 32 off: exp(-delta/T)=exp(-0.5), good. For far k 100 off exp(-? 100*16/n^2 /5e-7 ~? 100*1.95e-7/5e-7=38, underflow to 0). effective set ~<100. OK. Floor adds background.

def softmax_values(v):
   v=np.asarray(v,float)
   mask=np.isfinite(v)
   if not mask.all():
      v2=np.where(mask, v, -1000.0)
   else: v2=v
   vmax=v2.max()
   if not np.isfinite(vmax): return np.full(v.size,1/v.size)
   e=np.exp(np.clip(v2-vmax, -745, 709))
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: uniform
   return e/s
```

```
def lowdim_obj(c, n, beta, x, xp):
   c_safe = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0)
   v = np.interp(x, xp, c_safe)
   p = softmax_values(v)
   q=conv(p)
   qmax=q.max(); exparg=beta*(q-qmax); w...; obj=(qmax+logsumexp)/beta
   gp=2*cross(p,w)
   gv_p = p*(gp - dot(gp,p))
   # accumulate to controls
   mc=len(xp)-1
   idx=np.minimum((x*mc).astype(int), mc-1)
   t=x*mc - idx
   gc=np.zeros(m)
   np.add.at(gc, idx, (1-t)*gv_p)
   np.add.at(gc, idx+1, t*gv_p)
   return obj, gc
```

`score_key`.

`fft_conv_self`:
```
def fft_conv_self(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0: return np.array([0.0])
    if n == 1: return np.array([float(a[0]*a[0])])
    size = 2*n -1
    fsize = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(A*A))
    # clip tiny negatives
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c[:size]
```
Clipping all negatives to zero; if true negative due to roundoff ok. If all zero no.

`cross_corr`:
```
def cross_corr(a, w):
   n=len(a); L=len(w)
   if n==0 or L==0: return np.zeros(n)
   fsize=1 << (n+L-2).bit_length()
   A=fft(a,fsize); W=fft(w,fsize)
   c=np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:n]
   return c
```

`normalize_sum` and `score_normalized`:
```
def normalize_sum(x, S=1.0):
   x=np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
   x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
   x=np.clip(x,0,1e12)
   s=x.sum()
   if s<=1e-300: return np.ones_like(x)*S/x.size if x.size>0 else x
   return x*(S/s)
```
Use S=1.0; for final maybe S=1.0.

`score_normalized(p)`:
```
def score_normalized(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,float); n=p.size
   if n==0: return np.inf
   s=p.sum()
   if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.inf
   p = p/s
   q=fft_conv_self(p)
   qm=float(q.max())
   if not np.isfinite(qm): return np.inf
   return 2.0*float(n)*qm
```

`softmax_values` as above.

`add_log_noise(p, rng, sigma)`:
```
def add_log_noise(p, rng, sigma):
   n=len(p)
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   # ensure positive
   pth=np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
   s=pth.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
   z=np.log(pth/s)
   if sigma>0:
      z += rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
   z -= z.max()
   e=np.exp(np.clip(z, -745, 709))
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
   return e/s
```

`make_weights_exp` as above.

Resample function top-level.

`resample_to_length`:
```
def resample_to_length(a, n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).reshape(-1)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if a.size==0 or s<=1e-12:
      return np.ones(n)/n
   p=a/s
   if a.size == n:
      out=p.copy()
   else:
      x_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
      x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      out=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p, left=0.0, right=0.0)
      out=np.clip(out,0,None)
      s2=out.sum()
      if s2<=1e-12: out=np.ones(n)/n
      else: out/=s2
   return out
```

Generate code length:
```
def generate_initials(n, parent_p, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out=[]
    out.append(parent_p.copy())
    for sigma in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.5):
        out.append(add_log_noise(parent_p, rng, sigma))
    out.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    for A in (-3.0,-2.0,-1.0,-0.5,0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0):
        out.append(softmax_values(A*(x-0.5)))
    for A in (-4.0,-2.0,-1.0,1.0,2.0,4.0):
        out.append(softmax_values(A*x*(1.0-x)))
    for A in (-3.0,-1.5,1.5,3.0):
        out.append(softmax_values(A*x))
        out.append(softmax_values(A*(1.0-x)))
    for A in (-2.0,-1.0,1.0,2.0):
        out.append(softmax_values(A*(2.0*x-1.0)**2))
    for r in (1,2,3,4,6,8):
        for A in (-1.0,1.0):
            out.append(softmax_values(A*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*r*x)))
            out.append(softmax_values(A*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*r*x)))
    for A in (-2.0,2.0):
        for B in (-1.0,1.0):
            out.append(softmax_values(A*(x-0.5)+B*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*x)))
    # random fourier
    for R in (4,8,16):
       for amp in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
          v=np.zeros(n)
          for f in range(1,R+1):
             af=rng.normal(0.0, amp/(1.0+0.3*f))
             bf=rng.normal(0.0, amp/(1.0+0.3*f))
             v += af*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*f*x) + bf*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*f*x)
          out.append(softmax_values(v))
    # random control
    for m in (16,32,64):
       xp=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
       for amp in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
          vc=rng.normal(0.0, amp, m)
          v=np.interp(x, xp, vc, left=vc[0], right=vc[-1])
          out.append(softmax_values(v))
    return out
```
This is ~6+1+8+6+16+8+24+8+3*3*? random fourier 9 + random control 9 = ~104. OK.

def run_lad(...):
    start=time.monotonic()
    p=normalize_sum(p_init)
    q=fft_conv_self(p)
    cur=2*n*q.max()
    best=cur; best_p=p.copy()
    K=...
    ...
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_limit:
        it +=1
        cur = 2*n*float(q.max())
        if cur < best - ...: update
        if best < target: break
        if it % 500 ==0: print
        if no_improve > 3000: reset
        if no_improve > 1000: adjust K/floor
        weights=make_weights_exp(q,K,floor_eps)
        g=2*cross_corr(p,weights)
        d=...
        if scale too small: no_improve += 50; continue
        old_cur=cur
        accepted=False
        e=eta
        anneal=...
        for _ in range(5):
            p_c=...
            q_c=fft_conv_self(p_c)
            score_c=2*n*float(q_c.max())
            if score_c < old_cur - 1e-10 or (anneal>0 and score_c < old_cur + anneal):
                p=p_c; q=q_c; cur=score_c; accepted=True
                no_improve=0
                if score_c < best: best=score_c; best_p=p_c.copy()
                if score_c < old_cur - 1e-6:
                    eta = min(3.0, max(eta, e)*1.2)
                else:
                    eta = min(3.0, max(eta, e)*1.05)
                break
            e *=0.5
            if e < 0.02: break
        if not accepted:
            no_improve +=1
            eta = max(0.05, eta*0.7)
    return best_p,best
```

`smooth_score_obj_grad_z` if objective NaN: return (1e100, zeros). But zeros gradient may cause L-BFGS stop. Good.
```
def smooth_score_obj_grad_z(z,beta,n):
   p=softmax_values(z)
   q=fft_conv_self(p); qmax=q.max()
   if not finite: return inf, zeros
   exparg=beta*(q-qmax)
   exp_bc=np.exp(np.clip(exparg,-745,0))
   sum_exp=exp_bc.sum()
   if sum_exp<=0: sum_exp=1
   obj=(2.0*n*(qmax+np.log(sum_exp)))/beta
   w=exp_bc/sum_exp
   gp=(4.0*float(n))*cross_corr(p,w)
   dot=float(np.dot(gp,p))
   gz=p*(gp-dot)
   if not finite obj or gz: return 1e100, zeros
   return float(obj), gz
```
Check gp scale: cross_corr(p,w) ~1/n? Actually w sum1; for active lag e, cross ~p shifted, entries ~1/n, sum ~1. For weighted, ~1/n. Multiply 4n -> ~4. Good.

L-BFGS z update: `z` can drift; after each beta center `z-=z.mean()`.

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step-function height sequence minimizing
    2*n*max_k((a*a)[k]) / (sum(a)^2).

Method
------
The optimization is done mainly in probability scale p = a / sum(a), sum(p)=1.
The objective then is 2*n*max(p*p).  A fast multiplicative mirror-descent
algorithm is used in logarithmic coordinates:

    grad G(p) for active convolution lags is computed by FFT.
    log p <- log p - eta * normalized(gradients)
    p <- p * exp(...) / sum(...)

Active lags are selected by an exponential temperature based on the current
convolution profile.  Many diverse starts (parent sequence, analytic shapes,
Fourier/spline random shapes) are optimized, followed by a short L-BFGS polish
on a smoothed objective.  The best finite FFT-scored candidate is returned and
verified with evaluate_sequence().
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    scipy_minimize = None


def score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def logits_to_probs(v):
    """Stable softmax."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    finite = np.isfinite(v)
    if not finite.all():
        v2 = np.where(finite, v, -1000.0)
    else:
        v2 = v

    vmax = float(v2.max())
    if not np.isfinite(vmax):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)

    e = np.exp(np.clip(v2 - vmax, -745.0, 709.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def normalize_sum(x, S=1.0):
    """Clip to nonnegative finite values and scale sum to S."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return x
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0e12)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1.0e-300:
        return np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return x * (S / s)


def fft_conv_self(a):
    """Full linear convolution a * a by FFT, length 2n-1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0] * a[0])], dtype=np.float64)

    size = 2 * n - 1
    fsize = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))
    c = c[:size]
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c


def cross_corr(a, w):
    """
    Return c_i = sum_k w[k] * a[k-i], with out-of-range values treated as 0.
    This is the unnormalized derivative of lag-k convolution terms with
    weights w[k].
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    L = w.size
    if n == 0 or L == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    fsize = 1 << (n + L - 2).bit_length()
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    W = fft(w, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    return c


def score_normalized(p):
    """Return evaluate-equivalent score for p (any scale)."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    p = p / s
    q = fft_conv_self(p)
    qm = float(q.max())
    if not np.isfinite(qm):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * qm)


def make_weights_exp(q, K, floor_eps):
    """
    Exponential weights over convolution lags, effectively choosing an active
    set of size about K.  A small floor keeps all constraints slightly active.
    """
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    L = q.size
    if L <= 1:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / max(L, 1)

    qmax = float(q.max())
    if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 1.0e-300:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)

    K = int(max(1, min(K, L)))
    if K >= L:
        top_min = float(q.min())
    else:
        # value of the K-th largest element
        top_min = float(np.partition(q, L - K)[L - K])

    delta = qmax - top_min
    if not np.isfinite(delta) or delta <= 1.0e-15 * max(qmax, 1.0e-300):
        T = max(qmax / (100.0 * max(K, 1)), 1.0e-300)
    else:
        T = max(2.0 * delta, qmax / (100.0 * max(K, 1)), 1.0e-300)

    exparg = (q - qmax) / T
    w = np.exp(np.clip(exparg, -700.0, 0.0))
    if floor_eps > 0.0:
        w = w + float(floor_eps)

    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s


def add_log_noise(p, rng, sigma):
    """Perturb log(p), then renormalize."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1.0e-300)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.log(p / s)
    if sigma > 0.0:
        z = z + rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), n)
    z -= float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745.0, 709.0))
    s2 = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s2


def run_lad(p_init, time_limit, target_score, rng, tag="lad"):
    """
    Multiplicative log-space descent with adaptive active lag weights.
    The sequence is kept nonnegative and normalized to sum 1.
    """
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    time_limit = float(max(0.0, time_limit))
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        p = normalize_sum(p_init, 1.0)
        q = fft_conv_self(p)
        sc = float(2.0 * p.size * q.max()) if p.size else np.inf
        return p, sc

    p = normalize_sum(p_init, 1.0)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, np.inf

    q = fft_conv_self(p)
    cur = float(2.0 * n * q.max())
    best = cur
    best_p = p.copy()

    # Initial active-set width.  A few dozen lags is usually enough; it grows
    # if the iterate stalls.
    K = 32
    if n >= 16384:
        K = 64
    if n < 1024:
        K = 12

    floor_eps = 1.0e-4
    eta = 0.8
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - start_time < time_limit:
        it += 1

        qm = float(q.max())
        if not np.isfinite(qm):
            q = fft_conv_self(best_p)
            qm = float(q.max())
        cur = float(2.0 * n * qm)

        if cur < best - 1.0e-12:
            best = cur
            best_p = p.copy()

        if best <= target_score:
            break

        if it % 2000 == 0:
            print(
                "%s: it=%6d cur=%.8f best=%.8f eta=%.4f K=%d flr=%.0e st=%d t=%.1fs"
                % (tag, it, cur, best, eta, K, floor_eps, no_improve,
                   time.monotonic() - start_time)
            )

        # Stall escape: restart from best with log-noise.
        if no_improve > 3000:
            sigma = 0.10 + 0.05 * min(4, int((no_improve // 1000) - 2))
            p = add_log_noise(best_p, rng, sigma)
            q = fft_conv_self(p)
            cur = float(2.0 * n * q.max())
            no_improve = 1000
            eta = 0.8
            K = min(512, max(K, 32) * 2)
            floor_eps = min(1.0e-2, max(1.0e-5, floor_eps) * 2.0)
            continue

        if no_improve > 900:
            K = min(1024, K * 2)
            floor_eps = max(1.0e-6, floor_eps * 0.35)
        elif no_improve > 400 and floor_eps > 1.0e-3:
            floor_eps *= 0.7

        weights = make_weights_exp(q, K, floor_eps)
        g = 2.0 * cross_corr(p, weights)
        d = g - float(np.dot(g, p))
        d = d - float(d.mean())

        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d))) if d.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1.0e-14:
            no_improve += 100
            if no_improve > 3000:
                continue
            continue

        anneal = 0.0
        if no_improve > 250:
            anneal = min(1.0e-4, 1.0e-6 + 2.0e-8 * (no_improve - 250))

        old_cur = cur
        accepted = False
        e = eta

        for _ in range(5):
            arg = -e * d / scale
            p_c = p * np.exp(np.clip(arg, -50.0, 50.0))
            p_c = np.maximum(p_c, 1.0e-300)
            s_c = float(p_c.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s_c) or s_c <= 0.0:
                e *= 0.5
                if e < 0.02:
                    break
                continue
            p_c = p_c / s_c

            q_c = fft_conv_self(p_c)
            score_c = float(2.0 * n * q_c.max())

            if (score_c < old_cur - 1.0e-10) or (anneal > 0.0 and score_c < old_cur + anneal):
                p = p_c
                q = q_c
                cur = score_c
                accepted = True
                no_improve = 0
                if score_c < best - 1.0e-12:
                    best = score_c
                    best_p = p_c.copy()

                eta = min(3.0, max(0.05, e) * 1.10)
                break

            e *= 0.5
            if e < 0.02:
                break

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.70)

    return best_p, best


def resample_to_length(a, n):
    """Clip/nonnegative/rescale and interpolate to length n."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1.0e-12:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)

    p = a / s
    if a.size == n:
        out = p.copy()
    else:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p, left=0.0, right=0.0)
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, None)
        s2 = float(out.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 1.0e-12:
            out = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            out = out / s2
    return out


def generate_initials(n, parent_p, rng):
    """Construct diverse nonnegative probability starts."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = []

    out.append(parent_p.copy())
    p_sym = 0.5 * (parent_p + parent_p[::-1])
    p_sym = normalize_sum(p_sym, 1.0)
    out.append(p_sym)

    for sigma in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40, 0.80, 1.50):
        out.append(add_log_noise(parent_p, rng, sigma))

    out.append(np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64))

    # Simple analytic log-density shapes.
    for A in (-3.0, -2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        out.append(logits_to_probs(A * (x - 0.5)))

    for A in (-4.0, -2.0, -1.0, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        out.append(logits_to_probs(A * x * (1.0 - x)))

    for A in (-3.0, -1.5, 1.5, 3.0):
        out.append(logits_to_probs(A * x))
        out.append(logits_to_probs(A * (1.0 - x)))

    for A in (-2.0, -1.0, 1.0, 2.0):
        out.append(logits_to_probs(A * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2))

    for r in (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8):
        for A in (-1.0, 1.0):
            out.append(logits_to_probs(A * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * r * x)))
            out.append(logits_to_probs(A * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * r * x)))

    for A in (-2.0, 2.0):
        for B in (-1.0, 1.0):
            out.append(logits_to_probs(A * (x - 0.5) + B * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)))

    # Block-like log-densities.
    for w in (0.10, 0.20, 0.30):
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        v[np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0) < w] = 1.5
        out.append(logits_to_probs(v))

        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        v[np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0) > 1.0 - w] = 1.5
        out.append(logits_to_probs(v))

    # Random Fourier-like shapes.
    for R in (4, 8, 16):
        for amp in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for f in range(1, R + 1):
                scale = amp / (1.0 + 0.3 * float(f))
                af = rng.normal(0.0, scale)
                bf = rng.normal(0.0, scale)
                v = v + af * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x) + bf * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x)
            out.append(logits_to_probs(v))

    # Random control-point log-density shapes.
    for m in (16, 32, 64):
        xp = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
        for amp in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            vc = rng.normal(0.0, amp, m)
            v = np.interp(x, xp, vc, left=vc[0], right=vc[-1])
            out.append(logits_to_probs(v))

    return out


def smooth_score_obj_grad_z(z, beta, n):
    """
    Smoothed score surrogate for softmax parameters z:
        score(p) = 2 n max_k (p*p)[k]
    approximated by (2n/beta) logsumexp(beta*(p*p)).
    """
    p = logits_to_probs(z)
    q = fft_conv_self(p)
    qmax = float(q.max())
    zero_grad = np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 0.0:
        return 1.0e100, zero_grad

    exparg = beta * (q - qmax)
    exp_bc = np.exp(np.clip(exparg, -745.0, 0.0))
    sum_exp = float(exp_bc.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sum_exp) or sum_exp <= 0.0:
        sum_exp = 1.0

    obj = float((2.0 * n * (qmax + np.log(sum_exp))) / beta)
    w = exp_bc / sum_exp

    gp = (4.0 * float(n)) * cross_corr(p, w)
    dot = float(np.dot(gp, p))
    gz = p * (gp - dot)

    if not np.isfinite(obj) or not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
        return 1.0e100, zero_grad
    return obj, gz


def lbfgs_fun(z, beta, n):
    val, _ = smooth_score_obj_grad_z(z, beta, n)
    return float(val)


def lbfgs_jac(z, beta, n):
    _, grad = smooth_score_obj_grad_z(z, beta, n)
    return grad


def run_lbfgs_polish(p_init, time_limit, beta_schedule, maxiter):
    """Limited L-BFGS-B polish in softmax coordinates."""
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    time_limit = float(max(0.0, time_limit))

    p = normalize_sum(p_init, 1.0)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, np.inf

    best = score_normalized(p)
    best_p = p.copy()

    if scipy_minimize is None or time_limit < 30.0:
        return best_p, best

    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1.0e-300))
    z -= float(z.mean())

    for beta in beta_schedule:
        if time.monotonic() - start_time > max(10.0, time_limit - 10.0):
            break

        # Conservative function budget; each call does several FFTs.
        fun_budget = int(max(30, min(90, (time_limit - (time.monotonic() - start_time)) / 8.0)))
        miter = int(max(20, min(maxiter, fun_budget // 2)))

        try:
            res = scipy_minimize(
                lbfgs_fun,
                z,
                jac=lbfgs_jac,
                args=(beta, n),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": miter,
                    "maxfun": fun_budget,
                    "ftol": 1.0e-14,
                    "gtol": 1.0e-8,
                    "maxls": 20,
                    "iprint": -1,
                },
            )
            z = res.x
        except Exception as exc:
            print("L-BFGS polish error:", exc)
            break

        if z is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
            break

        p_new = logits_to_probs(z)
        p_new = normalize_sum(p_new, 1.0)
        sc = score_normalized(p_new)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best:
            best = sc
            best_p = p_new.copy()

        z = np.log(np.maximum(best_p, 1.0e-300))
        z -= float(z.mean())

        print("L-BFGS polish beta=%d local=%.8f best=%.8f elapsed=%.1f"
              % (beta, sc, best, time.monotonic() - start_time))

    return best_p, best


def propose_candidate():
    """
    Main search entry point.  Returns a list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

    # Leave a small margin for final verification/return.
    budget_s = 2380.0
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + budget_s

    # Use 8192 unless the provided parent has a reasonable length.
    parent_raw = None
    try:
        parent_raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        parent_raw = None

    if parent_raw is not None and parent_raw.size > 0:
        parent_len = int(parent_raw.size)
        n = 8192
        if 1024 <= parent_len <= 12288:
            n = parent_len
        # cap to keep FFT/optimization comfortably inside 1 CPU / 2400s
        n = int(min(n, 12288))
    else:
        n = 8192

    if parent_raw is None or parent_raw.size == 0:
        parent_p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        parent_p = resample_to_length(parent_raw, n)

    parent_p = normalize_sum(parent_p, 1.0)
    parent_score = score_normalized(parent_p)
    if not np.isfinite(parent_score):
        parent_p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        parent_score = score_normalized(parent_p)

    global_best_p = parent_p.copy()
    global_best_score = float(parent_score)

    print("=== Starting: n=%d parent_score=%.8f ===" % (n, global_best_score))
    print("Target: 1.5027, previous: 1.508805")

    reserve_final = 700.0 if budget_s > 1500.0 else 0.30 * budget_s
    candidate_deadline = deadline - reserve_final

    # Generate and preliminary-score a broad catalog of starts.
    initials = generate_initials(n, global_best_p, rng)
    scored_initials = []
    for p in initials:
        p = normalize_sum(p, 1.0)
        if p.size != n:
            continue
        sc = score_normalized(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored_initials.append((sc, p))

    scored_initials.sort(key=score_key)
    scored_initials = scored_initials[:40]

    for sc, p in scored_initials:
        if sc < global_best_score:
            global_best_score = sc
            global_best_p = p.copy()

    print("Best initial score=%.8f after catalog" % global_best_score)

    # Phase 1: parent-focused multiplicative log-space search.
    remaining_for_candidates = candidate_deadline - time.monotonic()
    parent_alloc = min(600.0, max(0.0, remaining_for_candidates - 350.0))
    if parent_alloc > 20.0:
        p_out, s_out = run_lad(
            global_best_p if global_best_score < parent_score else parent_p,
            parent_alloc,
            1.500,
            rng,
            tag="PARENT"
        )
        if np.isfinite(s_out) and s_out < global_best_score:
            global_best_score = float(s_out)
            global_best_p = p_out.copy()
            print("After parent search: %.8f" % global_best_score)

    # If the parent was not the best initial, also re-run the best catalog.
    if scored_initials and scored_initials[0][0] < parent_score - 1.0e-7:
        rem = candidate_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 60.0:
            alloc = min(300.0, rem)
            p_best, s_best = scored_initials[0]
            p_out, s_out = run_lad(p_best, alloc, 1.500, rng, tag="TOPINIT")
            if np.isfinite(s_out) and s_out < global_best_score:
                global_best_score = float(s_out)
                global_best_p = p_out.copy()
                print("After top initial search: %.8f" % global_best_score)

    # Phase 2: diversify with other catalog starts.
    alloc_list = [
        260, 160, 120, 90, 70, 55, 45, 35, 30, 25, 20, 18, 15, 12, 10,
        8, 8, 6, 6, 5, 5, 5, 5, 4, 4, 4, 4, 3, 3, 3,
    ]

    for idx, item in enumerate(scored_initials):
        sc_init, p_arr = item
        if time.monotonic() > candidate_deadline - 10.0:
            break

        # Avoid spending much time on very poor starts once a good best exists.
        if idx >= 8 and np.isfinite(sc_init) and sc_init > global_best_score + 0.35:
            continue

        alloc = float(alloc_list[min(idx, len(alloc_list) - 1)])
        rem = candidate_deadline - time.monotonic()
        alloc = min(alloc, rem)
        if alloc < 5.0:
            break

        # Lower-priority runs get less time when the board is small.
        if idx >= 15:
            alloc *= 0.75

        p_out, s_out = run_lad(
            p_arr,
            alloc,
            1.500,
            rng,
            tag="INIT%d" % idx
        )
        if np.isfinite(s_out) and s_out < global_best_score:
            global_best_score = float(s_out)
            global_best_p = p_out.copy()
            print("Candidate %2d improved to %.8f" % (idx, global_best_score))

    print("After LAD phase: best=%.8f" % global_best_score)

    # Phase 3: short L-BFGS polish on top of the best exact-LAD candidate.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 120.0:
        lb_time = min(350.0, 0.45 * remaining)
        p_out, s_out = run_lbfgs_polish(
            global_best_p,
            lb_time,
            beta_schedule=[2000, 8000, 30000],
            maxiter=45
        )
        if np.isfinite(s_out) and s_out < global_best_score:
            global_best_score = float(s_out)
            global_best_p = p_out.copy()
            print("After L-BFGS polish: %.8f" % global_best_score)

    # Phase 4: final LAD polish.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 60.0:
        final_alloc = max(20.0, remaining - 30.0)
        p_out, s_out = run_lad(
            global_best_p,
            final_alloc,
            1.500,
            rng,
            tag="FINAL"
        )
        if np.isfinite(s_out) and s_out < global_best_score:
            global_best_score = float(s_out)
            global_best_p = p_out.copy()
            print("After final LAD: %.8f" % global_best_score)

    # If search worsened the parent, fall back to parent.
    if np.isfinite(parent_score) and global_best_score > parent_score + 1.0e-8:
        global_best_p = parent_p.copy()
        global_best_score = float(parent_score)
        print("Falling back to parent because search was worse locally.")

    global_best_p = normalize_sum(global_best_p, 1.0)
    global_best_p = np.clip(global_best_p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if global_best_p.sum() <= 1.0e-12:
        global_best_p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)

    final_list = [float(x) for x in global_best_p]

    # Verify with the official evaluator if available.
    verified = False
    try:
        ver = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        if np.isfinite(ver):
            print("Official verification score: %.8f" % float(ver))
            verified = True
        else:
            print("Official verification returned inf; fallback")
    except Exception:
        verified = False

    if verified:
        return final_list

    # Fallbacks: original parent, then uniform.
    parent_list = [float(x) for x in parent_p]
    try:
        ver_p = evaluate_sequence(parent_list)
        if np.isfinite(ver_p):
            print("Parent fallback verification score: %.8f" % float(ver_p))
            return parent_list
    except Exception:
        pass

    uniform_list = [float(1.0 / n) for _ in range(n)]
    try:
        ver_u = evaluate_sequence(uniform_list)
        if np.isfinite(ver_u):
            return uniform_list
    except Exception:
        pass

    return uniform_list
```
